Hmm, OK, I think the issue might be something fundamental that I'm missing. Let me re-read the gameStep code one more time, very carefully.

```javascript
if (Inputs.col === undefined || Inputs.row === undefined) return;
```

If `Inputs.col` or `Inputs.row` is undefined, return. This could happen if the BoardCell doesn't report col/row. But it should.

```javascript
const CRATES = [[2, 2], [6, 2], [4, 3], [2, 5], [4, 5], [6, 6]];
const FISH_COL = 7;
const FISH_ROW = 1;

const isCrate = (col, row) => CRATES.some((c) => c[0] === col && c[1] === row);
```

Crates at (col,row): (2,2), (6,2), (4,3), (2,5), (4,5), (6,6). Fish at (7,1).

```javascript
let catCol = Inputs.catCol;
let catRow = Inputs.catRow;
let dogCol = Inputs.dogCol;
let dogRow = Inputs.dogRow;
let moves = Inputs.moves;
let message = Inputs.message;
let gameOver = Inputs.gameOver;
let tallyWon = Inputs.tallyWon;
let tallyLost = Inputs.tallyLost;
```

Read the current state from inputs.

```javascript
if (!gameOver) {
  const tapCol = Inputs.col;
  const tapRow = Inputs.row;
  const dCol = tapCol - catCol;
  const dRow = tapRow - catRow;
  const onBoard = tapCol >= 1 && tapCol <= 7 && tapRow >= 1 && tapRow <= 7;
  const nextToCat = Math.abs(dCol) + Math.abs(dRow) === 1;
```

Calculate the tap position relative to the cat.

```javascript
  const last = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem("lechonk_last_move") || "null");
  const now = Date.now();
  const slideCol = catCol + (last ? last.dCol : 0);
  const slideRow = catRow + (last ? last.dRow : 0);
  const slides = last && dCol === 0 && dRow === 0 && now - last.at < 700 &&
    slideCol >= 1 && slideCol <= 7 && slideRow >= 1 && slideRow <= 7 && !isCrate(slideCol, slideRow) &&
    !(slideCol === dogCol && slideRow === dogRow);
  sessionStorage.removeItem("lechonk_last_move");
```

Check for slide. If the tap is on the cat's current position (dCol=0, dRow=0) within 700ms of the last move, and the slide target is valid, it's a slide.

```javascript
  if (slides) {
    catCol = slideCol;
    catRow = slideRow;
    moves = moves + 1;
    message = "";
    if (catCol === FISH_COL && catRow === FISH_ROW) {
      gameOver = true;
      message = "Le Chonk got the fish! 🐟";
      tallyWon = tallyWon + 1;
      saveTally(tallyWon, tallyLost);
    }
  } else if (nextToCat && onBoard && !isCrate(tapCol, tapRow)) {
    sessionStorage.setItem("lechonk_last_move", JSON.stringify({ dCol: dCol, dRow: dRow, at: now }));
    catCol = tapCol;
    catRow = tapRow;
    moves = moves + 1;
    message = "";
    if (catCol === FISH_COL && catRow === FISH_ROW) {
      gameOver = true;
      message = "Le Chonk got the fish! 🐟";
      tallyWon = tallyWon + 1;
      saveTally(tallyWon, tallyLost);
    } else if (catCol === dogCol && catRow === dogRow) {
      gameOver = true;
      message = "Caught! The dog wins.";
      tallyLost = tallyLost + 1;
      saveTally(tallyWon, tallyLost);
    } else if (!Inputs.qaFrozen) {
      // The dog's answer: one step per move.
      for (let i = 0; i <= 1 && !gameOver; i++) {
        const next = dogStep(dogCol, dogRow, catCol, catRow);
        dogCol = next[0];
        dogRow = next[1];
        if (dogCol === catCol && dogRow === catRow) {
          gameOver = true;
          message = "Caught! The dog wins.";
          tallyLost = tallyLost + 1;
          saveTally(tallyWon, tallyLost);
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    message = "Le Chonk can only move one square";
  }
}
```

If it's a slide, move the cat. If it's a valid adjacent move, move the cat and the dog responds. Otherwise, set the error message.
